- Owner: repo-local workflow
- Note: registered `proposed` by owner roadmap steering (`2026-06-15`); **activated
  `2026-06-16` by explicit owner directive** ("deep semantic introspection shall
  be first-class … everything shall be queryable via MCP through a top-notch
  API"), taking priority over the table order per the PNT owner-names-a-lane rule.

## Goal

Deepen ANVIL's introspection surface from today's **structural / metric
projection** (the versioned `--introspect` envelope + MCP read-mostly tools,
`AGENT-INTROSPECTION-MCP` / `AGENT-MCP-EXPANSION`, decisions `0004`/`0005`) toward
a **behavioral query surface** — letting an agent ask derived/behavioral
questions about a generated artifact (e.g. "what cones depend on input X",
"which flops are reset-defined", "what is the support of output Y") beyond the
raw serde projection of `Config`/`Metrics`/`DesignMetrics`.

## Non-Goals

- No stateful simulator-style session API and no shadow simulator (the
  `agent-introspection-mcp-lane` boundary, decision `0004`; ROADMAP steering gap
  4 — structure-first, a full shadow simulator stays out of scope).
- No new computed truth that drifts from the generator's own facts: any
  behavioral query must be derived from existing IR / construction-time facts,
  not a second source of truth (the `SCHEMA-DERIVED` invariant, decision `0004`).
- No change to the default-off / DUT-byte-identical contract of the MCP/introspect
  lanes.

## Acceptance Criteria

- Each landed query is derived from existing IR/metrics (no drift, no second
  oracle), versioned in the introspection schema, and default-off / DUT
  byte-identical.
- Live docs + book (`agent-mcp.md`) + schema doc + a Knowledge Map fact per
  durable query surface.
- Every leaf committed through `COMMIT.md` with its leaf id.

(1) Result shape: a new NodeDrivers { node: u32, kind: String, op: Option<String>, width: u32, drivers: Vec<NodeRef> } + NodeRef { node: u32, kind: String, name: String } + an EIGHTH parallel vec node_drivers: Vec<NodeDrivers> on DerivedAnalysis (#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")] ⇒ the seven prior documents byte-identical); op uses #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")] (omitted for non-gate nodes); drivers are in OPERAND ORDER (not sorted — operand order distinguishes a-b from b-a; deterministic because the IR operand vec is). node-kind vocabulary: "primary_input"/"constant"/"flop_q"/"mem_read"/"fsm_out"/"instance_output"/"gate". NodeRef.name resolves each operand to a usable handle (input port name / "flop:<id>" / "mem:<id>" / "fsm:<id>" / "<instance>.<port>" / "node:<id>" for interior gates+constants), the lane's resolve-handles convention. (2) Derivation: a single pass over 0..m.nodes.len() (ascending = node id) reading one level of operands — no transitive walk / no DFS / no memoization (even more local than output_support); the fmt closure resolves an instance-output operand. Pure, no IR/generator change. (3) Addressing: "node:<id>" (a new vocabulary, parallel to "flop:<id>"/"mem:<id>"/"fsm:<id>"); None ⇒ all nodes ascending id (the whole node-level adjacency in one query — the flop_dependencies/module_reachability complete-graph-view precedent); a leaf node ⇒ one entry with empty drivers (known-but-empty); unknown/out-of-range (id >= nodes.len()) ⇒ no entry ⇒ -32602; the run_analyze empty-result guard checks analysis.node_drivers for this kind. (4) Module-vs-design: module_node_drivers + design_node_drivers on the top module (early-return empty when top absent), per-child-module a future extension; design variant uses format_instance_leaf_design so instance-output operands resolve to "<instance>.<child-output-port-name>". (5) Schema: additive MINOR 1.20 → 1.21, envelope reused, DUT byte-identical. Pre-split .9b → .9b.1 (pure core + the NodeDrivers/NodeRef types + the eighth node_drivers: Vec::new() fill-ins + lib proofs; NOT in supported_query_kinds yet) + .9b.2 (surface: registry + run_analyze dispatch in one commit + schema 1.20 → 1.21 + analyze_schema enum + schema-doc/book/USER_GUIDE/README/KM + e2e smoke). Docs/design only — no src/ ⇒ DUT byte-identical; self-checks green.`

- ID: `SEMANTIC-INTROSPECTION-EXPANSION.10`

With both queries an agent walks the construction DAG in either direction one hop at a time. The fifth query beyond decision 0011's four named kinds, under the lane's "open-ended breadth" clause; same SCHEMA-DERIVED / pure-post-hoc / default-off / DUT-byte-identical contract; a new "node_readers" query kind in the analyze registry.`

(1) Result shape: a new NodeReaders { node: u32, kind: String, op: Option<String>, width: u32, readers: Vec<NodeRef> } + a NINTH parallel vec node_readers: Vec<NodeReaders> on DerivedAnalysis (#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")] ⇒ the eight prior documents byte-identical); REUSES the existing NodeRef struct (.9) — one struct for both directions (full-factorization). node/kind/op/width describe the SUBJECT node (mirroring NodeDrivers field-for-field); op keeps #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]. readers are in ASCENDING reader node-id order — SORTED + DEDUPED (unlike node_drivers' operand order — a node's readers are a set; the x & x double-operand reader appears once). - ID: `SEMANTIC-INTROSPECTION-EXPANSION.11`
  Status: `done`
  Goal: `The tenth derived query — instance_provenance: for each child instance in a design's top, the support cone of each of the child module's output ports, built INSIDE the child module's own node graph. The THIRD opaque-leaf boundary-opener — completing the trilogy with memory_provenance (.7, opens Node::MemRead) and fsm_provenance (.8, opens Node::FsmOut): the third opaque leaf build_cone terminates a cone at is Node::InstanceOutput, and this query opens it by reporting what drives each child output inside the child. The FIRST and only derived query that crosses the module boundary (every prior query lives in one module's node graph), which is exactly why it is Design-only — a bare Module carries the InstanceOutput leaves but not the child module definitions needed to descend. The sixth query beyond decision 0011's four named kinds, under the lane's "open-ended breadth" clause; same SCHEMA-DERIVED / pure-post-hoc / default-off / DUT-byte-identical contract; a new "instance_provenance" query kind in the analyze registry.`
  Children: `SEMANTIC-INTROSPECTION-EXPANSION.11a`, `SEMANTIC-INTROSPECTION-EXPANSION.11b`
  Result: `Done (2026-06-24) — instance_provenance delivered end-to-end: .11a design-detail + .11b.1 pure core + .11b.2 surface (registry + run_analyze dispatch + schema 1.22 → 1.23 + analyze_schema enum + schema-doc/book/USER_GUIDE/README/KM + e2e anvil-mcp smoke). The MCP analyze tool answers query=instance_provenance with an InstanceProvenance per child instance (module/role + the support cone of each child output port built INSIDE the child module's graph, reusing build_cone unchanged with the child as its walked module); the nine prior queries stay byte-identical (instance_provenance omitted); unknown instance ⇒ -32602; module variant degenerate (no-child-defs). E2e: seed 42 hierarchy → schema 1.23, 2 instances; u_0/mod_42_0000/planned_child, cone u_0.o_0 → support_inputs ["i_1"] + support_flops [0] = the CHILD's input+flop across the module boundary. DUT byte-identical (snapshots 6/6). The tenth derived query — the sixth beyond decision 0011's four named kinds, the THIRD opaque-leaf boundary-opener (opens Node::InstanceOutput, the sibling of .7's MemRead + .8's FsmOut) and the first/only query crossing the module boundary — is delivered; the lane returns to a no-frontier boundary (stays active; further kinds — a per-FSM/per-memory reach, input-binding chaining, recursive multi-level descent — remain open-ended breadth, nothing retired).`

- ID: `SEMANTIC-INTROSPECTION-EXPANSION.13`
  Status: `done`
  Goal: `The twelfth derived query — longest_path: for a target (an output port, or a flop D cone addressed "flop:<id>"), one representative LONGEST combinational fan-in path — the ordered chain of interior Gate nodes (each with its GateOp) realizing the output_support cone's scalar cone_depth, terminating at a boundary leaf (a NodeRef). The WITNESS for output_support's scalar cone_depth and the TRANSITIVE COMPLEMENT to node_drivers (.9): output_support reports the SET of leaves + a scalar depth (collapsing every interior gate); node_drivers reports a single node's 1-hop operands; neither returns an ordered transitive chain of interior gates — longest_path does. Structural gate-depth, NOT timing (no delay model — the 0004/0011 structure-first ceiling; named longest_path not critical_path to keep that honest). Provable cross-query consistency: longest_path(t).depth == output_support(t).cone_depth. Single-endpoint (the exact output_support addressing — output port name / "flop:<id>" — so it fits analyze {query, target} with NO MCP signature change; a two-endpoint witness-path between a chosen source AND target is a recorded future kind). The eighth query beyond decision 0011's four named kinds, under the lane's "open-ended breadth" clause; same SCHEMA-DERIVED / pure-post-hoc / default-off / DUT-byte-identical contract; a new "longest_path" query kind in the analyze registry.`
  Children: `SEMANTIC-INTROSPECTION-EXPANSION.13a`, `SEMANTIC-INTROSPECTION-EXPANSION.13b`
  Result: `Done (2026-06-24) — longest_path delivered end-to-end: .13a design-detail + .13b.1 pure core + .13b.2 surface (registry + run_analyze dispatch + schema 1.24 → 1.25 + analyze_schema enum + schema-doc/book/USER_GUIDE/README/TOOLBOX/KM + e2e anvil-mcp smoke). The MCP analyze tool answers query=longest_path with one representative longest combinational fan-in path per target (the ordered interior-gate chain realizing output_support's cone_depth, terminating at a NodeRef leaf); the eleven prior queries stay byte-identical (longest_path omitted); unknown target ⇒ -32602. The twelfth derived query — the eighth beyond decision 0011's four named kinds, the witness for output_support's scalar cone_depth + the transitive complement to node_drivers, structural gate-depth NOT timing, provably longest_path(t).depth == output_support(t).cone_depth — is delivered; the lane returns to a no-frontier boundary (stays active; further kinds remain open-ended breadth, nothing retired). DUT byte-identical (snapshots 6/6).`

  Status: `done`
  Goal: `Design-detail leaf (no source): ground longest_path in the real Module/Node/GateOp IR + the resolve_target / visit (depth recurrence) / node_ref_of / gate_op_str machinery in analyze.rs. Pin the result shape (a TWELFTH parallel longest_path: Vec<LongestPath> vec + LongestPath { target, depth, path: Vec<PathStep>, leaf: Option<NodeRef> } + a minimal PathStep { node, op, width } — every step a Gate; REUSE the existing NodeRef for the terminal leaf), the derivation (two pure passes: a node_depth memo = the same recurrence visit returns, then a greedy max-child-depth descent with ties broken by smallest operand node id ⇒ a unique byte-stable path; NO graph mutation, NO IR field, NO generator change), single-endpoint addressing (the exact output_support namespace — output port name / "flop:<id>"; None ⇒ all outputs; resolvable-but-undriven ⇒ known-but-empty; unknown ⇒ -32602), module-vs-design (REAL in BOTH — the output_support pattern, only the instance-leaf fmt differs; not Design-only like .11), the depth == cone_depth consistency invariant, the structural-NOT-timing honesty boundary, and the schema bump (1.24 → 1.25). DEVELOPMENT_NOTES design-detail entry + the .13b impl shape. No new numbered decision (the .3a–.12a per-query precedent; decision 0011 governs the surface).`
  Acceptance: `A DEVELOPMENT_NOTES design-detail entry resolving the Q-points grounded in the real Module/Node/GateOp IR + the analyze.rs resolve_target/visit/node_ref_of/gate_op_str code; tree split recorded; no source change; docs/workflow self-checks clean.`
  Result: `Done. DEVELOPMENT_NOTES design-detail entry "longest_path impl design-detail — .13a" resolves all five Q-points (Q1 result shape, Q2 derivation, Q3 single-endpoint addressing, Q4 real-in-both module-vs-design, Q5 schema bump + pre-split) plus the genuinely-new framing (witness for cone_depth / transitive complement to node_drivers), the structural-NOT-timing honesty boundary, and the depth == cone_depth provable consistency, grounded in a fresh read of the Module/Node/GateOp IR + the landed resolve_target/visit/node_ref_of/gate_op_str/build_cone code in analyze.rs. (1) Result shape: LongestPath { target, depth, path: Vec<PathStep>, leaf: Option<NodeRef> } + PathStep { node, op, width } (every step a Gate ⇒ op always present, kind implicitly "gate" omitted) + a TWELFTH parallel vec longest_path: Vec<LongestPath> on DerivedAnalysis (#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")] ⇒ the eleven prior documents byte-identical); REUSES the existing NodeRef for the terminal leaf. (2) Derivation: resolve_target → root; a node_depth memo (0 for leaf/operand-less gate, 1 + max(child) for a gate — the recurrence visit returns); greedy descent picking the max-depth operand, ties → smallest operand node id (a unique byte-stable path); stop at the first non-gate = the leaf. Pure post-hoc; no IR field, no generator change; O(cone_nodes) + O(depth). (3) Addressing: the exact output_support namespace (output port name / "flop:<id>"); single-endpoint ⇒ NO MCP signature change; None ⇒ all outputs decl-order; resolvable-but-undriven ⇒ one result, empty path + leaf None (known-but-empty); unknown ⇒ -32602. (5) Schema: additive MINOR 1.24 → 1.25, envelope reused, DUT byte-identical. Pre-split .13b → .13b.1 (pure core + LongestPath/PathStep types + the twelfth longest_path: Vec::new() fill-ins + lib proofs incl. the depth == cone_depth consistency proof + the deterministic tie-break proof + leaf-classification proofs; NOT in supported_query_kinds yet) + .13b.2 (surface: registry + run_analyze dispatch in one commit + schema 1.24 → 1.25 + analyze_schema enum + schema-doc/book/USER_GUIDE/README/TOOLBOX/KM + e2e smoke). - ID: `SEMANTIC-INTROSPECTION-EXPANSION.14`
  Status: `done`
  Goal: `The thirteenth derived query — node_reach: for a target node addressed "node:<id>", the TRANSITIVE combinational fan-OUT of that node — the boundary sinks it reaches: the output ports it drives (reaches_outputs) and the flop D cones it feeds (reaches_flops), plus their total (fanout_targets). The TRANSITIVE COMPLEMENT to node_readers (.10) — completing the node-addressed driver/reader × 1-hop/transitive 2×2 matrix (node_drivers .9 = backward 1-hop, node_readers .10 = forward 1-hop, longest_path .13 = backward transitive, node_reach .14 = forward transitive) — and the node-addressed GENERALIZATION of input_reach (.3) that SUBSUMES the recorded per-FSM/per-memory reach candidate (address a MemRead/FsmOut node by kind; nothing retired). Reuses PathStep (no new step type); a new ReachPath { node, depth, path, sink } payload. Provable cross-query consistency: reach_path(n).sink.is_some() ⟺ node_reach(n).fanout_targets > 0, and the chosen sink ∈ node_reach(n)'s sink set; reach_path(n).depth == path.len(). Structural gate-depth NOT timing (the longest_path honesty boundary). The tenth query beyond decision 0011's four named kinds, under the lane's "open-ended breadth" clause; same SCHEMA-DERIVED / pure-post-hoc / default-off / DUT-byte-identical contract; a new "reach_path" query kind in the analyze registry. - ID: `SEMANTIC-INTROSPECTION-EXPANSION.15a`
  Status: `done`
  Goal: `Design-detail leaf (no source): ground reach_path in the real Module/Node/Flop IR + the node_reach_with reader-index transpose / build_longest_path greedy-descent / node_depth memo / driver_of_port / m.drives / m.flops machinery in analyze.rs. DEVELOPMENT_NOTES design-detail entry + the .15b impl shape. No new numbered decision (the .3a–.14a per-query precedent; decision 0011 governs the surface).`

  Result: `Done. DEVELOPMENT_NOTES design-detail entry "reach_path impl design-detail — .15a" resolves all five Q-points (Q1 result shape, Q2 derivation, Q3 "node:<id>" addressing + sink boundary, Q4 real-in-both module-vs-design, Q5 consistency + schema bump + pre-split) plus the genuinely-new framing (the forward-transitive WITNESS — the forward complement to longest_path .13 and the path-witness for node_reach .14, exactly as longest_path is the witness for output_support's cone_depth), grounded in a fresh read of the Module/Node/Flop IR + the landed node_reach_with reader-index transpose / build_longest_path greedy descent / node_depth memo / driver_of_port / node_ref_of / gate_op_str / node_kind_str code in analyze.rs. (1) Result shape: ReachPath { node: u32, depth: usize, path: Vec<PathStep>, sink: Option<String> } + a FOURTEENTH parallel vec reach_path: Vec<ReachPath> on DerivedAnalysis (#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")] ⇒ the thirteen prior documents byte-identical); REUSES PathStep (every step a Gate, the longest_path step type); sink in the existing output_support/longest_path target namespace (an output port name, or "flop:<id>"), None ⟺ the node reaches no boundary sink; the forward mirror of LongestPath (target→node, leaf:NodeRef→sink:String — a fan-out boundary is an output/flop, not a single IR node, and the target string re-addresses it for a follow-up longest_path/output_support). (2) Derivation: build the reader index (transpose operands — the node_reach_with pass); a SINK-AWARE forward gate-height memo gate_reach_height(g) = the gates on the longest forward chain from gate g to a sink-driving gate (inclusive), 0 ⟺ g reaches no sink — recurrence cont = max over reader-gate r of gate_reach_height(r); = if cont>0 {1+cont} else if drives_sink(g) {1} else {0}; memoized, reader graph is a DAG (flops/instances/mem/fsm are non-Gate leaves ⇒ no reader edges ⇒ no cycles); then a greedy descent from the entry gate (push a PathStep; h==1 ⇒ terminate at pick_sink(cur); else step to the reader gate with gate_reach_height==h-1, ties→smallest reader id ⇒ byte-stable). pick_sink(n) = smallest output-port name with driver==n, else "flop:<id>" of smallest flop whose d==n, else None (used for the terminal gate AND the direct-leaf case). Pure post-hoc; no IR field, no generator change (reads m.nodes/m.outputs/m.drives/m.flops + the operand graph only — the node_reach read set). (3) Addressing: "node:<id>" (the node_drivers/node_readers/node_reach namespace ⇒ NO MCP signature change); None ⇒ all nodes ascending id; reaches-nothing ⇒ known-but-empty (depth 0/empty path/sink None); non-"node:<id>" or out-of-range ⇒ -32602. Sink boundary symmetric with node_reach/input_reach (output ports + flop Ds only; an instance-input-only node reaches nothing; chain instance_input_bindings → instance_provenance to cross the boundary). (4) Module-vs-design: REAL in both (one module's node graph; no fmt closure — PathStep carries ids+ops and sinks are plain port names + flop ids; missing design top ⇒ empty analysis). (5) Consistency: reach_path(n).depth == path.len(); reach_path(n).sink.is_some() ⟺ node_reach(n).fanout_targets > 0 (the headline cross-check, the forward analog of longest_path.depth == output_support.cone_depth); the chosen sink ∈ node_reach(n)'s sink set; every path step lists the previous step (or the start node) as a direct operand and the last step drives the sink. ## Current Frontier

**No active frontier.** **`.15` (`reach_path`) is closed** (`.15a` design + `.15b.1` pure core +
`.15b.2` surface) — the lane returns to a no-frontier boundary (stays `active`; further kinds —
recursive multi-level instance descent, a two-endpoint witness-path, instance-input reach sinks —
remain open-ended breadth, none retired). **Fourteen** derived queries are now delivered end-to-end @
introspection schema `1.27`, DUT byte-identical.

**`.15` (`reach_path`) — closed.** The **fourteenth** derived query and the **forward-transitive
witness**: for a start node addressed `"node:<id>"`, one representative **longest combinational
fan-OUT path** — the ordered chain of interior `Gate` nodes (each a `PathStep` with its `GateOp`)
from the node forward to a boundary **sink** (an output port, or a flop `D` as `"flop:<id>"`). It is
the **forward complement to `longest_path` (`.13`)** (the backward fan-in witness) and the
**path-witness for `node_reach` (`.14`)** (the forward fan-out *set*): `node_reach` reports *which*
sinks a node reaches; `reach_path` reports the longest gate-chain *to* one of them — exactly as
`longest_path` is the gate-chain realizing `output_support`'s scalar `cone_depth`. It is
**single-endpoint** `"node:<id>"` (the `node_reach` namespace ⇒ **no MCP signature change**) and the
`sink` is expressed in the existing `output_support`/`longest_path` target namespace (an output port
name, or `"flop:<id>"`), so `reach_path(n).sink` chains straight into `longest_path`/`output_support`.
It **reuses `PathStep`** (no new step type) + a new `ReachPath { node, depth, path, sink }` payload —
the forward mirror of `LongestPath { target, depth, path, leaf }` (`target → node`, `leaf:NodeRef →
sink:String`). Provable consistency: `reach_path(n).depth == path.len()`, `reach_path(n).sink.is_some()
⟺ node_reach(n).fanout_targets > 0`, and the chosen `sink ∈ node_reach(n)`'s sink set. Structural
gate-depth **NOT** timing (the `longest_path` honesty boundary). The **tenth query beyond decision
`0011`'s four named kinds**, under the lane's "open-ended breadth" clause; schema `1.26 → 1.27` at
impl; decision `0011` governs (no new numbered decision). Pre-split `.15b` → `.15b.1` (pure core) +
`.15b.2` (surface). Docs/design only ⇒ DUT byte-identical.

node-addressed **generalization of `input_reach` `.3`** that **subsumes** the recorded
per-FSM/per-memory reach candidate (address a `MemRead`/`FsmOut` node by `kind`; nothing retired).

input_reach(i).{…}` (verified live in the `.14b.2` e2e smoke — 0 mismatches). Sinks are output
ports + flop `D`s only (symmetric with `input_reach`). Schema `1.26`; SCHEMA-DERIVED /
pure-post-hoc / default-off / DUT byte-identical; no new numbered decision (decision `0011`
governs; the `.3a`–`.13a` per-query precedent).

**No active frontier** *(superseded — historical, prior to `.14a`)*. **`.13` (`longest_path`) is closed** (`.13a` design + `.13b.1` pure core +
`.13b.2` surface) — the lane returns to a no-frontier boundary (stays `active`; further kinds —
a per-FSM/per-memory reach, recursive multi-level instance descent, a two-endpoint witness-path —
remain open-ended breadth, none retired). `longest_path` returns, for a
target (an output port / `"flop:<id>"`), **one representative longest combinational fan-in
path** — the ordered chain of interior `Gate` nodes (each with its `GateOp`) realizing the
`output_support` cone's scalar `cone_depth`, terminating at a boundary leaf (a `NodeRef`). It is
the **witness for `output_support`'s scalar `cone_depth`** and the **transitive complement to
`node_drivers` (`.9`)**: `output_support` reports the leaf *set* + a scalar depth (collapsing
interior gates), `node_drivers` reports one node's 1-hop operands, and neither carries an ordered
transitive chain of interior gates — `longest_path` does. It is **structural gate-depth, NOT
timing** (no delay model — the `0004`/`0011` structure-first ceiling; named `longest_path` not
`critical_path` to keep that honest), with the provable consistency
`longest_path(t).depth == output_support(t).cone_depth`. It is **single-endpoint** (the exact
`output_support` addressing — output port name / `"flop:<id>"` — so it fits `analyze {query,
target}` with **no MCP signature change**; a two-endpoint witness-path is a recorded future kind).
Real in **both** module and design variants (the `output_support` pattern, only the instance-leaf
fmt differs; not Design-only like `.11`). The **eighth beyond decision `0011`'s four named
kinds**, under the lane's "open-ended breadth" clause; schema bump `1.24 → 1.25` at impl;
decision `0011` governs (no new numbered decision). Pre-split `.13b` → `.13b.1` (pure core) +
`.13b.2` (surface). Docs/design only ⇒ DUT byte-identical.

---

**No active frontier** *(superseded — historical, prior to `.13a`)*. **`.12` (`instance_input_bindings`) is closed** (`.12a` design + `.12b.1`
pure core + `.12b.2` surface) — the lane returns to a no-frontier boundary (stays `active`).
`instance_input_bindings` is the **eleventh** derived query and the **parent-side dual of
`instance_provenance` `.11`** (the exact future extension `.11a` named and deferred): for each
child instance in a design's top, the **parent node** (`NodeRef`) driving each child **input**
port, read from `Instance.inputs: Vec<(PortId, NodeId)>`. `.11` descends INTO the child for each
output; `.12` ascends to the PARENT for each input — together they bracket the instance boundary
on both sides, and chaining `.12 → .11` traces a parent signal across the module boundary through
a child output and back. Unlike `.11` (Design-only, degenerate module variant), `.12`'s module
variant is **non-degenerate** — the parent driver is resolvable in a bare module's own graph; only
the child input port *name* needs the child def (fallback `"port<id>"`). The **seventh beyond
decision `0011`'s four named kinds**, under the lane's "further derived-query kinds are open-ended
breadth" clause; schema `1.24`; decision `0011` governs (no new numbered decision). The `.12b.2`
e2e `anvil-mcp` smoke found-and-corrected a control-port doc bug (a clocked child's `clk`/`rst_n`
ARE bound in `Instance.inputs`; the code already reported them — only the prose was wrong). The
tree stays `active` (nothing retired): further kinds — recursive multi-level instance descent, a
per-FSM/per-memory reach — remain open-ended breadth (none named as a blocker).

**Twelve** derived queries are now delivered end-to-end — `output_support` (`.1`/`.2`),
`input_reach` (`.3`), `flop_reset_provenance` (`.4`), `module_reachability` (`.5`),
`flop_dependencies` (`.6`), `memory_provenance` (`.7`), `fsm_provenance` (`.8`), `node_drivers`
(`.9`), `node_readers` (`.10`), `instance_provenance` (`.11`), `instance_input_bindings`
(`.12`), and `longest_path` (`.13`) — at introspection schema
`1.25`, DUT byte-identical.

## Decisions

- `2026-06-24` (`.14a`, design-detail in `DEVELOPMENT_NOTES.md`): pinned `node_reach`, the
  **thirteenth** derived query — *for a node addressed `"node:<id>"`, the transitive combinational
  fan-OUT of that node: the boundary sinks it reaches (`reaches_outputs` output ports +
  `reaches_flops` flop `D` cones + `fanout_targets`)* — and the **ninth query beyond decision
  `0011`'s four named kinds** (after `flop_dependencies` `.6`, `memory_provenance` `.7`,
  `fsm_provenance` `.8`, `node_drivers` `.9`, `node_readers` `.10`, `instance_provenance` `.11`,
  `instance_input_bindings` `.12`, `longest_path` `.13`), under the lane's "open-ended breadth"
  clause (decision `0011`'s API + the `0004`/`0011` SCHEMA-DERIVED ceiling; **no new numbered
  decision**, the `.3a`–`.13a` per-query precedent). It **completes the node-addressed driver/reader
  × 1-hop/transitive 2×2 matrix** — `node_drivers` `.9` (backward 1-hop), `node_readers` `.10`
  (forward 1-hop), `longest_path` `.13` (backward transitive), `node_reach` `.14` (the missing
  forward-transitive corner) — and is the **transitive complement to `node_readers`** (one hop → the
  whole forward closure), mirroring how `longest_path` is the transitive complement to
  `node_drivers`. It is the node-addressed **generalization of `input_reach` `.3`** that **subsumes**
  the recorded per-FSM/per-memory reach candidate (address a `MemRead`/`FsmOut` node by `kind`;
  **nothing retired** — the per-FSM/per-memory reach is delivered more generally). Result shape: a
  thirteenth parallel `node_reach: Vec<NodeReach>` vec (`#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if)]` ⇒
  twelve prior documents byte-identical), `NodeReach { node, kind, op, width, reaches_outputs,
  reaches_flops, fanout_targets }` (node-family header field-for-field with `NodeReaders` +
  `input_reach` payload field-for-field with `ReachResult`; **no new leaf type**). Derivation: build
  the reader index by transposing operands (the `node_readers_with` pass), forward-closure BFS from
  the target node, classify sinks = output ports whose driver ∈ closure + flops whose `D` ∈ closure;
  the **register boundary is automatic** (a flop is not a `Gate` ⇒ no reader edge ⇒ the walk never
  crosses `D`→`Q`); **no graph mutation, no IR field, no generator change** (the `coverage_gaps` /
  `node_readers` project-don't-recompute precedent). Addressing: `"node:<id>"` (the
  `node_drivers`/`node_readers` namespace ⇒ **no MCP signature change**; None ⇒ all nodes ascending
  id; reaches-nothing ⇒ known-but-empty; unknown/out-of-range ⇒ `-32602`). Sink boundary **symmetric
  with `input_reach`**: output ports + flop `D`s only (an instance-input-only node reaches nothing;
  chain `instance_input_bindings` → `instance_provenance` to cross the boundary; instance-input reach
  a future extension). Module-vs-design: **real in both** (one module's node graph; no fmt closure —
  sinks are plain names+ids; missing design top ⇒ empty analysis). Provable cross-query consistency:
  `node_reach("node:<PrimaryInput-of-i>").{reaches_outputs,reaches_flops} == input_reach(i).{…}`.
  Schema `1.25 → 1.26` (additive MINOR). Pre-split `.14b` → `.14b.1` (pure core) + `.14b.2`
  (surface).

- `2026-06-24` (`.12a`, design-detail in `DEVELOPMENT_NOTES.md`): pinned
  `instance_input_bindings`, the **eleventh** derived query — *for each child instance in a
  design's top, the parent node (`NodeRef`) driving each of the child's **input** ports* —
  and the **seventh query beyond decision `0011`'s four named kinds** (after
  `flop_dependencies` `.6`, `memory_provenance` `.7`, `fsm_provenance` `.8`, `node_drivers`
  `.9`, `node_readers` `.10`, `instance_provenance` `.11`), under the lane's "open-ended
  breadth" clause (decision `0011`'s API + the `0004`/`0011` SCHEMA-DERIVED ceiling; **no new
  numbered decision**, the `.3a`–`.11a` per-query precedent). It is the **parent-side dual of
  `instance_provenance` (`.11`)** — the exact future extension `.11a` named and deferred
  (*"map a child-input support leaf back to the parent node that drives it through
  `Instance.inputs`"*). `.11` **descends** into the child for each output; `.12` **ascends** to
  the parent for each input — together they bracket the instance boundary on both sides, and
  chaining `.12 → .11` traces a parent signal across the module boundary. Result shape: an
  eleventh parallel `instance_input_bindings: Vec<InstanceInputBindings>` vec
  (`#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if)]` ⇒ ten prior documents byte-identical), the
  `InstanceInputBindings { instance, module, role, input_bindings: Vec<InstanceInputBinding> }`
  + `InstanceInputBinding { port, driver: NodeRef }` structs **reusing the existing `NodeRef`**
  for the parent driver. Derivation: a **pure read** of `Instance.inputs: Vec<(PortId, NodeId)>`
  — driver = `node_ref_of(top, parent_node_id, top_fmt)` (parent-side; a child input bound to a
  sibling instance output resolves to `"<sibling>.<port>"`), the child def used **only** to name
  the child input port (fallback `"port<id>"`); **no graph walk, no IR field, no generator
  change** (the `coverage_gaps` project-don't-recompute precedent). Addressing: the child
  instance **name** (None ⇒ all instances ascending name; bindings ascending child PortId; an
  instance with no data bindings known-but-empty; unknown ⇒ `-32602`). Module-vs-design: a
  **NON-degenerate** module variant — the load-bearing contrast with `.11` — the parent driver
  lives in the bare module's own graph, so `module_instance_input_bindings` carries real
  bindings; only the child port *name* degrades to `"port<id>"`. Schema `1.23 → 1.24` (additive
  MINOR). Pre-split `.12b` → `.12b.1` (pure core) + `.12b.2` (surface). 1-hop scope (chain
  `output_support`/`node_drivers` on `driver.node` for the transitive parent cone — the
  `node_drivers` one-hop precedent; nothing retired).

- `2026-06-24` (`.11a`, design-detail in `DEVELOPMENT_NOTES.md`): pinned
  `instance_provenance`, the **tenth** derived query — *for each child instance in a
  design's top, the support cone of each child output port built **inside the child
  module's graph*** — and the **sixth query beyond decision `0011`'s four named kinds**
  (after `flop_dependencies` `.6`, `memory_provenance` `.7`, `fsm_provenance` `.8`,
  `node_drivers` `.9`, `node_readers` `.10`), under the lane's "open-ended breadth"
  clause (decision `0011`'s API + the `0004`/`0011` SCHEMA-DERIVED ceiling; **no new
  numbered decision**, the `.3a`–`.10a` per-query precedent). It is the **third
  opaque-leaf boundary-opener** — completing the trilogy with `memory_provenance` (`.7`,
  `Node::MemRead`) and `fsm_provenance` (`.8`, `Node::FsmOut`): the three opaque leaves
  `build_cone` terminates a cone at are `MemRead`, `FsmOut`, and `InstanceOutput`, and
  `instance_provenance` opens the third by reporting **what drives each child output
  inside the child**. Uniquely it is the **first and only derived query that crosses the
  module boundary** (every prior query lives in a single module's node graph), which is
  exactly why it is **Design-only**: a bare `Module` carries the `InstanceOutput` leaves
  but **not** the child module definitions needed to descend; the `module_*` variant is
  the degenerate no-child-defs case (instances with empty cones), the
  `format_instance_leaf_module` / `module_module_reachability` precedent. Result shape: a
  tenth parallel `instance_provenance: Vec<InstanceProvenance>` vec
  (`#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if)]` ⇒ nine prior documents byte-identical), the
  `InstanceProvenance { instance, module, role, output_support: Vec<SupportCone> }` struct
  **reusing `SupportCone`** (the `memory_provenance`/`fsm_provenance` cone-reuse
  precedent). Derivation: **reuse `build_cone` unchanged** — only the `Module` it walks
  changes (the child, not the top). Instance-**name** addressing; schema additive MINOR
  `1.22 → 1.23`. Scope boundary (future, nothing retired): no input-binding chaining
  (`Instance.inputs`) + descends exactly one level (grand-child outputs are cone leaves).
  Pre-split `.11b` → `.11b.1` (pure core) / `.11b.2` (surface).

- `2026-06-23` (`.8a`, design-detail in `DEVELOPMENT_NOTES.md`): pinned
  `fsm_provenance`, the **seventh** derived query — *per-generated-encoding-FSM
  provenance* — and the **third query beyond decision `0011`'s four named kinds**
  (after `flop_dependencies` `.6` and `memory_provenance` `.7`), under the lane's
  "open-ended breadth" clause (decision `0011`'s API + the `0004`/`0011`
  SCHEMA-DERIVED ceiling; **no new numbered decision**, the
  `.3a`/`.4a`/`.5a`/`.6a`/`.7a` per-query precedent). It is the **direct sibling of
  `memory_provenance`**: the query that **opens the documented opaque-`Node::FsmOut`-leaf
  boundary**, exactly as `.7` opened the `Node::MemRead` boundary. The six prior
  queries terminate the combinational cone at a `Node::FsmOut` (an opaque registered
  leaf, recorded in no support list); `fsm_provenance` surfaces what drives the FSM's
  **one generated input port**, the transition-select cone `sel`. It deliberately
  does **not** surface the transition/output table *values* (the
  construction-time-resolved state-machine behaviour — that would drift toward a
  behavioural oracle, the `0004`/`0011` non-goal; the FSM analog of `memory_provenance`
  not recursing through a memory's stored contents). (1) Result shape: a new
  `FsmProvenance { fsm: u32, num_states, encoding: String, state_width, sel_width,
  out_width, is_mealy: bool, sel_support: SupportCone }` + a SEVENTH parallel vec
  `fsm_provenance: Vec<FsmProvenance>` on `DerivedAnalysis`
  (`#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]` ⇒ the six prior documents
  byte-identical); the one `sel` cone reuses the existing tested `SupportCone` (target
  `"fsm:<id>.sel"`); `is_mealy` is the FSM analog of `MemoryProvenance::single_port`.
  (2) Derivation: `build_cone` over `Fsm::sel` (a plain `NodeId` ⇒ `Some(node)`) via
  the same machinery `output_support` uses + project the structural fields
  (`state_width = FsmEncoding::state_width(num_states)`, the existing impl method);
  ascending fsm id; pure, no IR/generator change. (3) Addressing: `"fsm:<id>"` (a new
  vocabulary parallel to `"mem:<id>"` / `"flop:<id>"` / the module name); `None` ⇒ all
  FSMs ascending; unknown/out-of-range ⇒ `-32602`; FSM-less + `None` ⇒ empty (default-off
  `fsm_prob` ⇒ DUT byte-identical regardless). (4) Module-vs-design: `module_fsm_provenance`
  + `design_fsm_provenance` on the top module (`format_instance_leaf_design` fmt for
  resolved instance-output leaves). (5) Schema: additive MINOR `1.19 → 1.20`, envelope
  reused. Pre-split `.8b` → `.8b.1` (pure core) + `.8b.2` (surface).

- `2026-06-23` (`.7a`, design-detail in `DEVELOPMENT_NOTES.md`): pinned
  `memory_provenance`, the **sixth** derived query — *per-inferrable-memory port
  provenance* — and the **second query beyond decision `0011`'s four named kinds**
  (after `flop_dependencies` `.6`), under the lane's "open-ended breadth" clause
  (decision `0011`'s API + the `0004`/`0011` SCHEMA-DERIVED ceiling; **no new
  numbered decision**, the `.3a`/`.4a`/`.5a`/`.6a` per-query precedent). It is the
  query that **opens the documented opaque-`MemRead`-leaf boundary**: the five prior
  queries terminate the combinational cone at a `Node::MemRead` (an opaque registered
  leaf, recorded in no support list — the `analyze.rs` docs' named "future kind");
  `memory_provenance` surfaces what drives the memory's **input ports** (it still
  does not recurse *through* a memory's stored contents — that is a register
  boundary, like a flop `Q`). (1) Result shape: a new `MemoryProvenance { mem: u32,
  addr_width, data_width, kind: String, single_port: bool, read_addr_support:
  SupportCone, write_addr_support: SupportCone, write_data_support: SupportCone,
  write_enable_support: SupportCone }` + a **sixth** parallel vec `memory_provenance:
  Vec<MemoryProvenance>` on `DerivedAnalysis` (`#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if
  = "Vec::is_empty")]` ⇒ the five prior documents stay byte-identical); each port
  reuses the existing tested `SupportCone` (one cone shape, one walker —
  full-factorization), `target = "mem:<id>.<port>"`. A `SinglePort` memory shares one
  address node ⇒ `read_addr_support == write_addr_support` by construction (flagged by
  `single_port`, not deduplicated — ship the complete shape for the agent consumer).
  (2) Derivation: `build_cone` per port over the existing IR graph (the
  `we`/`waddr`/`wdata`/`raddr` cones are plain `NodeId`s, never `None`); project the
  structural fields; ascending mem id; pure, no IR/generator change; a port cone
  reaching another memory's `MemRead` or an `FsmOut` terminates at that opaque leaf
  for free (finite/acyclic); the `format_instance_leaf_*` fmt closure is needed (a
  memory port cone CAN depend on a child-instance output). (3) Addressing:
  `"mem:<id>"` (a new vocabulary, parallel to `"flop:<id>"` / the module name); `None`
  ⇒ all memories ascending id; unknown/out-of-range ⇒ no entry ⇒ `-32602`; memoryless
  + `None` ⇒ empty (default-off `memory_prob` ⇒ the default DUT has no memories ⇒
  honest empty answer, DUT byte-identical regardless). (4) Module-vs-design:
  `module_memory_provenance` + `design_memory_provenance` on the **top** module
  (early-return empty when top absent), per-child-module a future extension; the
  design variant resolves instance-output support to
  `"<instance>.<child-output-port-name>"`. (5) Schema: additive MINOR `1.18 → 1.19`,
  `DerivedAnalysisDocument` envelope reused, DUT byte-identical. Deferred sub-kinds
  (nothing retired): the **FSM** analog `fsm_provenance` (the `sel` transition-select
  cone behind the opaque `FsmOut` leaf) is the sibling `.8`; a per-memory **reach** is
  a further follow-up. Pre-split `.7b` → `.7b.1` (pure core) + `.7b.2` (surface); the
  registry entry + `run_analyze` dispatch land together in `.7b.2`.
- `2026-06-23` (`.6a`, design-detail in `DEVELOPMENT_NOTES.md`): pinned
  `flop_dependencies`, the **fifth** derived query — *the register-to-register
  (flop→flop) dependency graph* — and the **first query beyond decision `0011`'s
  four named kinds**, exercising the lane's documented "further derived-query kinds
  are open-ended breadth" clause (under decision `0011`'s API + the `0004`/`0011`
  SCHEMA-DERIVED ceiling; **no new numbered decision**, the `.3a`/`.4a`/`.5a`
  per-query precedent). The register-level analog of `module_reachability` (a graph
  over a node class), but reusing the existing **gate-graph** support/reach
  machinery rather than the module table. (1) Result shape: a new `FlopDependencies
  { flop: u32, depends_on_flops: Vec<u32>, driven_flops: Vec<u32>, self_dependent:
  bool }` + a **fifth** parallel vec `flop_dependencies: Vec<FlopDependencies>` on
  `DerivedAnalysis` (`#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]` ⇒
  the four prior documents stay byte-identical); both edge vecs present for every
  flop (sorted/deduped), no in/out-degree count fields (= vec `len`, a second source
  of truth), `self_dependent` the one genuinely-extra derived boolean (the
  self-feedback marker, parallel to `module_reachability.reachable`). (2) Derivation:
  reuse the support/reach machinery — `depends_on_flops` = the flop's D-cone
  `support_flops`; `driven_flops` = the transpose (one inversion pass, exactly
  `input_reach_with` restricted to flops); `self_dependent` = `flop ∈
  depends_on_flops`; pure, no IR/generator change; dual-consistency a free, provable
  test invariant; opaque `MemRead`/`FsmOut` terminate the underlying cone for free.
  Honest framing: each edge is individually derivable from `output_support` /
  `input_reach` on a `"flop:<id>"` target, but no single query returns the whole
  register graph — per the `2026-06-16` agent-API steering ("ask one query, get the
  complete machine-actionable answer") this is the register-graph **view**, not new
  computed truth. (3) Addressing: `"flop:<id>"` (consistent with `output_support` /
  `input_reach` / `flop_reset_provenance`); `None` ⇒ all flops ascending id;
  unknown/out-of-range ⇒ no entry ⇒ `-32602`; flopless + `None` ⇒ empty. (4)
  Module-vs-design: `module_flop_dependencies` + `design_flop_dependencies` on the
  **top** module (early-return empty when top absent), per-child-module a future
  extension — the `flop_reset_provenance` "operates on the top module" convention;
  no `format_instance_leaf_*` fmt closure needed (reports flop ids only). (5)
  Schema: additive MINOR `1.17 → 1.18`, `DerivedAnalysisDocument` envelope reused,
  DUT byte-identical. Deferred sub-kinds (nothing retired): a transitive
  register-reachability closure, SCC/feedback-loop grouping, a sequential-depth
  metric. Pre-split `.6b` → `.6b.1` (pure core) + `.6b.2` (surface); the registry
  entry + `run_analyze` dispatch land together in `.6b.2`.
- `2026-06-16` (`.5a`, design-detail in `DEVELOPMENT_NOTES.md`): pinned
  `module_reachability`, the **fourth** derived query (the last named kind in
  decision `0011`). (1) Result shape: a new `ModuleReachability { module,
  reachable, depth: Option<usize>, instantiates: Vec<String>, instance_count }` +
  a **fourth** parallel vec `module_reachability: Vec<ModuleReachability>` on
  `DerivedAnalysis` (`#[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]` ⇒
  the three prior documents stay byte-identical) — the established parallel-vec
  pattern (`.3a` rejected a tagged `results` enum precisely so each new kind is one
  more skip-if vec the `query` field discriminates). `depth` is `Option<usize>`
  with `skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none"`: present (0 = top) iff reachable.
  (2) Derivation: a BFS from `design.top` over the `Module.instances[].module`
  edges of a name→`Module` index — pure, min-depth, deterministic (output sorted by
  module name); no IR field, no generator change (the `coverage_gaps` /
  `output_support` project-don't-recompute precedent). (3) Addressing: `target` = a
  **module name** (`None` ⇒ every module sorted; `Some(name)` ⇒ that one; unknown ⇒
  no entry ⇒ `-32602`) — deliberately distinct from the prior queries' port-name /
  `"flop:<id>"` targets, because the module name is this query's natural
  identifier. (4) Module-vs-design: `design_module_reachability` is the real query;
  `module_module_reachability` degenerates to a single entry for the bare module
  itself (a one-node graph rooted at itself) since a bare `Module` carries no child
  defs to traverse — the same "no child defs" boundary the module variant of the
  other three queries hits. (5) Schema: additive MINOR `1.6 → 1.7`,
  `DerivedAnalysisDocument` envelope reused unchanged, DUT byte-identical.
  Pre-split `.5b` → `.5b.1` (pure core) + `.5b.2` (surface); the registry entry +
  `run_analyze` dispatch land together in `.5b.2` to keep every commit coherent.
- `2026-06-16` (owner steering, audience): **the introspection / MCP query API is
  for AI agents, not human consumption.** Agents can ingest and act on a lot
  of structured data very fast, so the API should optimize for **machine-friendly
  completeness, structured/queryable shape, batch breadth, and speed** — not
  human-readable minimalism or terse summaries. Design implication for every query
  kind (incl. `input_reach`, `.3`): prefer returning the full structured relation
  (all targets / complete reach sets / explicit ids) over abridged human digests;
  keep results JSON-structured and deterministic; lean into "ask one query, get the
  complete machine-actionable answer" rather than paginating for human eyes. This
  does **not** relax the SCHEMA-DERIVED / no-shadow-simulator ceiling — it is about
  *shape and completeness for the agent consumer*, still pure relations over the
  emitted IR. (Big results still spill to `ResourceRef` per `0011` to avoid
  unbounded inline payloads — a transport choice, not a completeness cut.)
- `2026-06-16` (owner steering, lane order): after the cross-module sequential
  equivalence sub-tree (`IDENTITY-DEEPENING.3b.2b`) closed, the owner directed PNT
  into this lane's next derived query, **`input_reach`** (`.3`).
- `2026-06-16` (`.2a`, design-detail in `DEVELOPMENT_NOTES.md`): resolved decision
  `0011`'s three open questions. (1) `DerivedAnalysis { query, results:
  Vec<SupportCone> }` + `SupportCone { target, support_inputs[], support_flops[],
  support_instance_outputs[], cone_nodes, cone_depth }` (serde + `Default`, sorted
  Vecs ⇒ deterministic) in a new pure `src/introspect/analyze.rs`;
  `module_support_cones(m, target: Option<&str>)` does a memoized DFS over the
  existing IR graph (no IR field / no generator change). `query`-kind:
  `output_support` first; `target` = output port name (absent ⇒ all outputs);
  unknown query/target ⇒ `-32602`. (2) The **default `introspect` payload stays
  lean** (no `analysis` field) — the cone is reached only via a new **pure** MCP
  `analyze` tool returning a standalone `DerivedAnalysisDocument` (envelope reuse,
  schema `1.3`); big cones inline first-cut (ResourceRef spill-over a noted `.2b`
  option). (3) The cone **stops at the instance boundary** (child-instance
  outputs are support leaves; recursion is a future kind). Schema `1.2 → 1.3`
  MINOR bump (DUT `.sv` byte-identical — introspect not in snapshots). Split `.2`
  → `.2a` (done) + `.2b` (impl); pre-split `.2b` → `.2b.1` (analyze module) /
  `.2b.2` (MCP tool + schema + docs) if broad.
- `2026-06-16` (`.1`, decision [`0011`](../decisions/0011-semantic-introspection-derived-query-surface.md)):
  activated the lane by explicit owner directive. The surface is a first-class,
  versioned, MCP-queryable, **SCHEMA-DERIVED derived-relation** API (a
  `DerivedAnalysis` introspection section, schema `1.3`, + a pure MCP `analyze`
  tool) answering *what depends on what* over the already-emitted IR by pure
  graph traversal — **relations, not behaviour** (the `0004` no-shadow-simulator
  / structure-first boundary is the permanent ceiling). First query = the output
  **support cone**. API = a fixed, extensible registry of named query kinds
  (prompts-registry pattern); big results are `ResourceRef`s. Default-off / DUT
  byte-identical (pure post-hoc, the `coverage_gaps` project-don't-recompute
  precedent). Rejected: behavioral/simulation queries, a free-form query
  language, a second source of truth, inlining whole cones, gen-time computation.
- `2026-06-15`: Registered `proposed` by owner roadmap steering as a named future
  capability lane. Not started; `SV-VERSION-TARGETING` was activated first.

## Open Questions

- Which derived/behavioral query is highest-leverage first while staying
  `SCHEMA-DERIVED` — resolved by `.1` when activated.

## Blockers

- None (not active by choice, not dependency).

  vec-aware `-32602` guard; `SCHEMA_VERSION 1.23 → 1.24` (17 assertion bumps); the

  76 → 77); CODEBASE_ANALYSIS (wired + schema history). The **e2e `anvil-mcp` stdio smoke
  found-and-corrected a control-port doc bug**: a clocked child's `clk`/`rst_n` ARE bound in
  `Instance.inputs` (the `.12a` design assumed otherwise) — the code already reported them
  correctly; only the prose was fixed. The MCP `analyze` tool now answers **eleven** derived
  queries; the lane returns to a no-frontier boundary.

- `2026-06-24`: **`.10b.1` landed — the pure `node_readers` core**

  (reusing the existing `NodeRef`) + the ninth `DerivedAnalysis.node_readers`
  `skip_serializing_if` vec (⇒ the eight prior documents byte-identical) +
  `module_node_readers`/`design_node_readers` + the `node_readers_with` driver: one
  pass building a `BTreeMap<u32, BTreeSet<u32>>` reader index by **transposing each
  `Gate`'s operands** — the BTreeSet keeps readers sorted+deduped+deterministic and
  collapses the `x & x` double-operand case to one reader; each reader resolved via the
  shared `node_ref_of`; output-port/flop-`D` drives are not operand edges so they are
  out of scope ⇒ the exact transpose, `B ∈ drivers(A) ⇔ A ∈ readers(B)`. The 15
  existing empty-fill `DerivedAnalysis` literals gained `node_readers: Vec::new()` (a
  guarded python insert skipping the two literals already carrying it).
  `supported_query_kinds()` unchanged (joins with the `run_analyze` dispatch in
  `.10b.2`). 5 new in-crate proofs (the exact-transpose proof both directions + the
  empty-readers boundary + the subject mirror; `x & x` dedup; `"node:<id>"`
  target/unknown; serialization omits the other eight vecs; design top-module variant).
  `cargo test --lib introspect::analyze` 58/0; `cargo test --lib` 676/0/2; snapshots
  6/6 byte-identical; clippy `-D warnings` + fmt clean; `CODEBASE_ANALYSIS.md` amended

  gate `op` (for a `Gate`), and the list of its direct operand `NodeRef`s (each
  operand's id + kind + resolved handle) in operand order. The **atomic node-level
  primitive complementing the transitive `output_support` cone** — where the cones
  collapse to leaves and never surface interior gates or their ops, `node_drivers`
  exposes the node-level fan-in graph one hop at a time **and** surfaces each node's
  `GateOp` (genuinely new information no prior query carries). The **fourth query
  beyond decision `0011`'s four named kinds**, under the lane's "open-ended breadth"
  clause; its dual `node_readers` (immediate fan-out) is a natural future sibling
  (nothing retired). DEVELOPMENT_NOTES design-detail entry resolves Q1–Q5 grounded in
  the real `Node`/`GateOp` IR + `analyze.rs`; pre-split `.9b` → `.9b.1` (pure core) +
  `.9b.2` (surface, schema `1.20 → 1.21`). No new numbered decision (the `.3a`–`.8a`
  per-query precedent; decision `0011` governs). `scripts/check_doctrines.sh` green;
  no `src/` touched ⇒ DUT byte-identical; frontier → `.9b.1`.
